# cppreference

cppreference.com: the C and C++ languages and standard libraries, offline HTML book converted to Markdown. Version 2025-02, from <https://github.com/PeterFeicht/cppreference-doc/releases/tag/v20250209> at v20250209, commit 744e05d554d42a5a58422b4d209d76552eb2d6ff.
License: CC-BY-SA-3.0 OR GFDL-1.3-or-later, by The cppreference.com contributors; see THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md. Each file is one official HTML page converted to Markdown: its text, headings, lists, tables and code kept, the site navigation left out.

This index is long: search it for your words, never read it whole. Then read one section of one file.
